Benchmark accuracy back-test

Out-of-sample validation on completed projects (delivery date passed): the predicted rate uses only POs that existed before each budget was created; the actual rate is what was really paid after. No peeking. Uncovered lines (no prior purchase history at the time) are excluded from the score and counted honestly below.
